Status: install ok installed
Priority: optional
Section: utils
Installed-Size: 122
Maintainer: Ubuntu Developers <ubuntu-devel-discuss@lists.ubuntu.com>
Architecture: amd64
Version: 2.3.1-1
Depends: libc6 (>= 2.38)
Description: displays an indented directory tree, in color
 Tree is a recursive directory listing command that produces a depth indented
 listing of files, which is colorized ala dircolors if the LS_COLORS environment
 variable is set and output is to tty.

[?2004l]133;C\]666;vte.shell.preexec!\[?1049h[22;0;0t[55;1H[?1h=[4mTREE[24m(1)                                                                                                General Commands Manual                                                                                                [4mTREE[24m(1)[m
[m
[1mNAME[0m[m
       tree - list contents of directories in a tree-like format.[m
[m
[1mSYNOPSIS[0m[m
       [1mtree  [22m[[1m-acdfghilnpqrstuvxACDFJQNSUX[22m]  [[1m-L  [4m[22mlevel[24m  [[1m-R[22m]]  [[1m-H  [22m[-][4mbaseHREF[24m] [[1m-T [4m[22mtitle[24m] [[1m-o [4m[22mfilename[24m] [[1m-P [4m[22mpattern[24m] [[1m-I [4m[22mpattern[24m] [[1m--gitignore[22m] [[1m--gitfile[22m[[1m=[22m][4mfile[24m] [[1m--matchdirs[22m] [[1m--metafirst[22m] [[1m--ignore-case[22m] [[1m--nolinks[22m] [[1m--hin‐[0m[m
       [1mtro[22m[[1m=[22m][4mfile[24m] [[1m--houtro[22m[[1m=[22m][4mfile[24m] [[1m--inodes[22m] [[1m--device[22m] [[1m--sort[22m[[1m=[22m][4mname[24m] [[1m--dirsfirst[22m] [[1m--filesfirst[22m] [[1m--filelimit[22m[[1m=[22m][4m#[24m] [[1m--si[22m] [[1m--du[22m]  [[1m--prune[22m]  [[1m--charset[22m[[1m=[22m][4mX[24m]  [[1m--timefmt[22m[[1m=[22m][4mformat[24m]  [[1m--fromfile[22m]  [[1m--fromtabfile[22m]  [[1m--fflinks[22m][m
       [[1m--info[22m] [[1m--infofile[22m[[1m=[22m][4mfile[24m] [[1m--noreport[22m] [[1m--hyperlink[22m] [[1m--scheme[22m[[1m=[22m][4mschema[24m] [[1m--authority[22m[[1m=[22m][4mhostname[24m] [[1m--opt-toggle[22m] [[1m--acl[22m] [[1m--selinux[22m] [[1m--condense[22m] [[1m--compress[22m[[1m=[22m][4m#[24m] [[1m--version[22m] [[1m--help[22m] [[1m--[22m] [[4mdirectory[24m ...][m
[m
[1mDESCRIPTION[0m[m
       [4mTree[24m  is  a  recursive directory listing program that produces a depth indented listing of files, which is colorized ala [4mdircolors[24m if the [1mLS_COLORS [22menvironment variable is set and output is to tty.  With no arguments, [4mtree[0m[m
       lists the files in the current directory.  When directory arguments are given, [4mtree[24m lists all the files and/or directories found in the given directories each in turn.  Upon  completion  of  listing  all  files/directories[m
       found, [4mtree[24m returns the total number of files and/or directories listed.[m
[m
       By default, when a symbolic link is encountered, the path that the symbolic link refers to is printed after the name of the link in the format:[m
[m
           name -> real-path[m
[m
       If the `[1m-l[22m' option is given and the symbolic link refers to an actual directory, then [4mtree[24m will follow the path of the symbolic link as if it were a real directory.[m
[m
[1mOPTIONS[0m[m
       [4mTree[24m understands the following command line switches:[m
[m
[1mLISTING OPTIONS[0m[m
       [1m-a     [22mAll files are printed.  By default tree does not print hidden files (those beginning with a dot `.').  In no event does tree print the file system constructs `.' (current directory) and `..' (previous directory).[m
[m
       [1m-d     [22mList directories only.[m
[m
       [1m-l     [22mFollows symbolic links if they point to directories, as if they were directories. Symbolic links that will result in recursion are avoided when detected.[m
[m
       [1m-f     [22mPrints the full path prefix for each file.[m
[m
       [1m-x     [22mStay on the current file-system only.  Ala [1mfind [4m[22m-xdev[24m[1m.[0m[m
[m
       [1m-L [4m[22mlevel[0m[m
              Max display depth of the directory tree.[m
[m
       [1m-R     [22mRecursively cross down the tree each [4mlevel[24m directories (see [1m-L [22moption), and at each level outputting to a file named [1m00Tree.html [22m(ala [1m-o[22m).[m
[m
       [1m-P [4m[22mpattern[0m[m
              List  only those files that match the wild-card [4mpattern[24m.  You may have multiple -P options. Note: you must use the [4m-a[24m option to also consider those files beginning with a dot `.' for matching.  Valid wildcard opera‐[m
              tors are `*' (any zero or more characters), `**` (any zero or more characters as well as null /'s, i.e. /**/ may match a single /), `?' (any single character), `[...]' (any single character listed  between  brackets[m
              (optional  -  (dash) for character range may be used: ex: [A-Z]), and `[^...]' (any single character not listed in brackets) and `|' separates alternate patterns. A '/' at the end of the pattern matches directories,[m
              but not files.[m
[m
       [1m-I [4m[22mpattern[0m[m
              Do not list those files or directories that match the wild-card [4mpattern[24m.  You may have multiple -I options.  See [4m-P[24m above for information on wildcard patterns.[m
[m
       [1m--gitignore[0m[m
              Uses git [1m.gitignore [22mfiles for filtering files and directories. Also attempts to walk up the directory tree from the given path(s) searching for [1m.gitignore [22mfiles as it goes, stopping at [1m/ [22mor a directory containing  a[m
              [1m.git [22mdirectory. If a [1m.git [22mdirectory is found it will add the [1m.git/info/exclude [22mfile as well. Also uses [1m$GIT_DIR/info/exclude [22mif present.[m
[m
       [1m--gitfile[22m[[1m=[22m][4mfile[0m[m
[7m Manual page tree(1) line 1 (press h for help or q to quit)[27m[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K              Use [4mfile[24m explicitly as a gitignore file.[m
[7m Manual page tree(1) line 2 (press h for help or q to quit)[27m[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K[m
[7m Manual page tree(1) line 3 (press h for help or q to quit)[27m[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K       [1m--ignore-case[0m[m
[7m Manual page tree(1) line 4 (press h for help or q to quit)[27m[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K              If a match pattern is specified by the [1m-P [22mor [1m-I [22moption, this will cause the pattern to match without regard to the case of each letter.[m
[7m Manual page tree(1) line 5 (press h for help or q to quit)[27m[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K[m
[7m Manual page tree(1) line 6 (press h for help or q to quit)[27m[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K       [1m--matchdirs[0m[m
[7m Manual page tree(1) line 7 (press h for help or q to quit)[27m[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K              If a match pattern is specified by the [1m-P [22moption, this will cause the pattern to be applied to directory names (in addition to filenames).  In the event of a match on the directory name, matching is disabled for the[m
[7m Manual page tree(1) line 8 (press h for help or q to quit)[27m[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K              directory's contents. If the [1m--prune [22moption is used, empty folders that match the pattern will not be pruned.[m
[7m Manual page tree(1) line 9 (press h for help or q to quit)[27m[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K[m
[7m Manual page tree(1) line 10 (press h for help or q to quit)[27m[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K       [1m--metafirst[0m[m
[7m Manual page tree(1) line 11 (press h for help or q to quit)[27m[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K              Print the meta-data information at the beginning of the line rather than after the indentation lines.[m
[7m Manual page tree(1) line 12 (press h for help or q to quit)[27m[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K[m
[7m Manual page tree(1) line 13 (press h for help or q to quit)[27m[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K       [1m--prune[0m[m
[7m Manual page tree(1) line 14 (press h for help or q to quit)[27m[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K              Makes tree prune empty directories from the output, useful when used in conjunction with [1m-P [22mor [1m-I[22m.  See [1mBUGS AND NOTES [22mbelow for more information on this option.[m
[7m Manual page tree(1) line 15 (press h for help or q to quit)[27m[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K[m
